Method: 在28种配置网格上进行系统蒸馏实验（Gemma-2-9B教师蒸馏至SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct和OLMo-2-1B-Instruct），区分BBQ-disambig和BBQ-ambig两类任务，引入silence-loss和filled-silence两个细粒度指标，审计四个训练语料库中的拒绝-作为-答案形状数据，提出PCCD三步诊断协议（拒绝校准、上下文遵循、能力保持）。

Result: 蒸馏将最偏基线的上下文覆盖错误率从44%降至24%，但在模糊任务中15%原本正确拒答的项目被改为刻板印象答案。OLMo-2上silence-loss为8%，filled-silence占新偏见的89%。两个效应不相关（Spearman ρ=0.19），且训练语料中拒绝-作为-答案形状占比<0.5%。SFT拒绝注入导致琐碎拒绝者失效模式（拒绝率99.8%，精度0.2%）。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏对偏见的影响是不可约地不对称的：它沿能力维度改善模型，同时沿校准维度造成伤害，且两个效应源于不同机制。聚合指标具有欺骗性，PCCD协议能有效捕捉这些被遗漏的危害。

Abstract: We show that knowledge distillation in small instruction-tuned language models has asymmetric effects on bias. On unambiguous tasks (BBQ-disambig), response-based distillation from a Gemma-2-9B teacher improves context-following: for the most biased baseline (SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct), it cuts the context-overriding error rate from 44% to 24%. On ambiguous tasks (BBQ-ambig), the same distillation destroys per-item refusal calibration: 15% of items where the baseline correctly abstained instead receive stereotype answers, even when overall refusal rate is preserved. The pattern reproduces on a second student family (OLMo-2-1B-Instruct), with silence-loss of 8% and filled-silence accounting for 89% of new bias. Across the full 28-configuration grid, the magnitudes of silence-loss and filled-silence are uncorrelated (Spearman $ρ=0.19$, n.s.), indicating that the two effects arise from distinct mechanisms.
  Aggregate stereotype metrics (CrowS-Pairs, overall BBQ Stereotype Reliance Score) average over both effects and conceal the per-item harm. We trace the calibration loss to a data-side mechanism: an audit of four training corpora finds <0.5% refusal-as-answer-shape. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with refusal injection either breaks parsing or over-corrects into a trivial-refuser regime (refusal rate 99.8%, disambig accuracy 0.2%) that aggregate metrics would call perfectly calibrated. We propose Per-Condition Calibration Diagnosis (PCCD), a three-step protocol that evaluates refusal calibration, context-following, and capability preservation. PCCD catches both the asymmetric harm and the trivial-refuser failure mode that aggregate evaluations miss.

</details>


### [104] [PTP: Previous-Token Prediction based LLM Inversion for Near-Exact Prompt Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29378)
*Pirzada Suhail,Nagasai Saketh Naidu,Atanu R Sinha,Amit Sethi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在黑盒环境下对大语言模型进行功能反转的方法：直接在目标LLM自身生成的合成数据上，从头训练一个显式的逆语言模型，采用"前一个token预测"的训练方式（与正向的下一个token预测形成对称），实现忠实且多样化的提示重建，无需访问模型权重或外部数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过自回归方式逐token生成文本，导致提示与响应之间存在多对多的映射关系，使得从观测到的输出反推提示变得困难。先前的LLM反转工作将提示恢复视为语义重建任务，依赖在大型外部数据集上微调预训练序列到序列模型，且需要访问模型权重或logits，这在实际黑盒场景中不可行。

Method: 核心方法是从头训练一个逆语言模型（Inverse Language Model），训练数据完全由目标LLM自身合成生成，无需外部辅助数据。与正向的下一个token预测对应，逆模型使用"前一个token预测"（previous-token prediction）进行训练，建立正向与逆向过程之间的生成性联系。通过采样，逆模型天然支持多样化的提示重建，且所有重建出的提示在正向目标LLM下都能诱导出相似的响应。

Result: 该方法在不同数据集上具有泛化能力，并且在用不同LLM生成的响应重建提示时展现出可迁移性。在基于token的评估指标（针对提示和响应重建）上，该方法优于先前的工作。

Conclusion: 本文展示了一种纯黑盒、无需辅助工具和外部数据的LLM功能反转方法，通过"前一个token预测"的逆向训练范式，实现了比先前基于语义重建的方法更优的提示重建性能，并具备跨模型迁移能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate text by auto-regressively sampling the next token. This inherently leads to a many-to-many mapping between prompts and responses, complicating the task of inferring prompts from observed outputs. Prior work on LLM inversion frames prompt recovery as a semantic reconstruction task. They rely on fine-tuning pretrained sequence-to-sequence models on large external datasets--and requiring access to model weights or logits--to generate semantically plausible prompts. In contrast, we present a functional approach to inverting a given LLM in a black-box setting, without auxiliary aids. We train an explicit inverse language model entirely from scratch on data synthetically generated from the target LLM itself. Analogous to forward next-token prediction, our inverse model is trained using previous-token prediction, establishing a generative link between the forward and inverse processes that enables faithful prompt reconstruction. Moreover, it naturally supports diverse prompt reconstructions through sampling, whereby all such prompts induce similar responses under the forward, target LLM. Our approach generalises across datasets and exhibits transferability in reconstructing prompts from responses generated by different LLMs. Further, across the set of token based evaluation metrics for prompt and response reconstructions, our approach outperforms prior work.

</details>


### [105] [Studying quantization trade-offs for efficient inference deployment in machine translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29397)
*Jim Zhao,Sohir Maskey,Koen Oostermeijer,Douglas Orr,Teryn Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大规模语言模型在真实服务器环境下部署时的量化权衡问题，通过对比EuroLLM和Hy-MT2两个翻译模型家族（1.7B到22B共5个模型）在A100/H100 GPU上的表现，发现将文档分块策略与W4A8或W8A8量化结合可以改善延迟-吞吐量帕累托曲线，同时引入WMT24++文档级评估，揭示标准句级评估无法预测量化与长上下文翻译之间的交互，其中Hy-MT2对量化稳健而EuroLLM对量化高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型在真实服务器环境中部署面临低延迟和高吞吐量的挑战，量化虽常用于减少内存占用和提升推理效率，但现有研究很少在受控的编排级工作负载下评估量化对延迟和吞吐量的实际影响，且标准MT基准仅依赖孤立句子，无法捕捉长上下文动态。

Method: 对EuroLLM和Hy-MT2两个翻译模型家族的5个模型（1.7B至22B）在单张A100或H100 GPU上进行量化权衡研究，结合文档分块策略与W4A8、W8A8量化格式评估延迟-吞吐量帕累托曲线；引入基于WMT24++的文档级评估框架，考察文本分块策略在量化条件下对翻译质量的影响。

Result: 文档分块策略与W4A8或W8A8量化结合能改善延迟-吞吐量帕累托曲线；标准段级评估无法预测量化和长上下文文档翻译之间的交互；Hy-MT2在量化下保持稳健，而EuroLLM对量化高度敏感，所有量化格式下翻译质量均迅速崩溃。

Conclusion: 推理效率与翻译质量之间的权衡不仅取决于量化格式，还取决于文本分块策略的选择，强调了在真实部署场景中综合考虑量化与分块策略的重要性。

Abstract: Deploying large language models in realistic server environments poses challenges, as the system needs to provide high-quality responses with low latency. Quantization is a common approach to reduce the memory footprint and improve inference efficiency, yet its impact on latency and throughput is rarely evaluated under controlled, orchestration-level workloads. In this work we study the quantization trade-offs of two translation model families, EuroLLM \citep{martins2025eurollm} and Hy-MT2 \citep{zheng2026hy} across five models ranging from 1.7B to 22B for efficient deployment on a single A100 or H100 GPU. We demonstrate that combining a document-chunking strategy with W4A8 or W8A8 quantization improves the latency-throughput Pareto-curve under a wide range of workloads. Furthermore, since standard machine translation (MT) benchmarks rely on isolated sentences and fail to capture long-context dynamics, we introduce a document-level evaluation from WMT24++ to assess how text chunking strategies affect translation quality under quantization. Our results reveal that standard segment-level evaluation can fail to predict the interaction between quantization and long-context document translation. While Hy-MT2 remains robust under quantization, EuroLLM shows strong sensitivity and translation quality collapses rapidly for all considered quantization formats. Overall, our experiments show that the trade-off between inference efficiency and translation quality depends not only on the quantization format, but also on the choice of text chunking strategy.

</details>


### [106] [Know It, Act on It: Investigating Memory Utilization in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29433)
*Zhaoxin Feng,Jianfei Ma,Emmanuele Chersoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解耦评估范式，将Know（记忆回忆）和Act（行为应用）测试配对施测于同一用户偏好，以隔离LLM智能体在记忆与应用之间的断裂。大规模实验发现智能体常常能回忆起用户偏好但在行为场景中未能应用该偏好，且在健康与治疗类偏好上尤其严重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体作为个性化伴侣需要记忆能力，但存在知识利用问题：即使用户偏好在上下文中完全存在，模型也可能无法依据其行动。关键在于无法区分模型是"没记住"还是"记住了但没用"，这阻碍了对记忆系统失效环节的精准定位与改进。

Method: 提出解耦评估范式，对同一用户偏好施配对的Know测试（回忆/记忆测试）和Act测试（行为应用测试）。在16个系统、5种记忆架构上进行大规模实验，评估1,000条嵌入在不同表达强度（三级）的用户偏好。

Result: Know与Act结果之间存在显著差距：智能体常通过回忆测试却在配对的行为场景中未能反映该偏好。记忆架构虽然缩小了这一差距，但在健康和治疗相关偏好上，行为应用能力仍然非常薄弱——而恰恰这些偏好的应用失败具有最大的现实危害。

Conclusion: LLM智能体的记忆系统存在"记住了但不会用"的结构性问题，记忆架构只能部分缓解。未来需关注从"记忆存储"到"行为应用"的转化能力提升，尤其在高风险领域（如健康与治疗）应优先优化。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents evolve into personalized companions, memory has emerged as a core capability. However, LLMs face a knowledge utilization problem: they may fail to act on relevant user preferences even when they are fully present in context. When an agent fails to tailor its response in a context where previously shared user preferences should matter, it is unclear whether the model failed to remember that information or remembered it but failed to use it. To isolate this breakdown, we introduce a decoupled evaluation paradigm that administers paired Know and Act tests to the same user preference. We conduct large-scale experiments across 16 systems and five memory architectures, evaluating 1,000 preferences embedded at three levels of expression strength. Our results show a large gap between Know and Act outcomes: agents often pass the recall test for a user preference but fail to reflect that same preference in the paired behavioral scenario. While memory architectures reduce this gap, utilization remains especially weak for health and therapy-related preferences, where failures to act carry the greatest real-world stakes.

</details>


### [107] [Evidence-Type Competition: When Can Interventional Data Teach Language Models Causal Direction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29484)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过完全受控的合成环境检验"干预数据是教模型因果推理的金标准"这一假设，发现该假设在辛普森悖论世界中失败：增加干预样本比例并不能改善因果方向判断，模型会从观测上下文复制符号。因果能力储存在权重中，而切换机制存在于上下文中，通过激活修补定位到中间层的观测行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干预数据（interventional data）被广泛认为是训练模型因果推理能力的黄金标准，但这一假设从未在完全受控环境中被严格检验。作者希望在辛普森悖论世界中系统性地对比观测相关性与因果效应，探究干预数据是否真的能教会模型正确的因果方向判断。

Method: 在合成环境中构造观测相关性与因果效应符号系统性相反的辛普森悖论世界；操纵预训练数据中干预样本的比例、推理时的上下文证据类型（纯观测/混合/干预探针）；采用内容操控（四状态）、跨训练种子一致性检验、参数规模扩展至0.93B、激活修补定位、符号随机化重训练等实验范式；并设计了证据平均化协议来量化采样噪声底限。

Result: 干预样本比例增加仅使do()响应幅度单调增长，符号仍复制自观测上下文；纯观测上下文在29/50世界中产生符号反转，混合上下文19/50，对齐干预探针41/50正确；移除观测证据后因果插值能力立即释放（ratio_true=+0.56）；11/11强反转跨种子稳定，0.93B规模下反转率31.8% vs 探针组6%；外部审计发现两层结构的正效应先验，符号随机化仅能移除分布内先验；证据平均化协议将符号错误从26%降至9%。

Conclusion: 模型的因果推理能力存于权重之中，但是否启用该能力由推理时的上下文证据类型决定，而非训练数据中的干预样本比例。干预数据的训练价值被高估，需要重新审视因果推理评估协议，并认识到上下文线索对模型因果判断方向的关键性调控作用。

Abstract: Interventional data is widely regarded as the gold standard for teaching models causal reasoning. We test this assumption in a fully controlled synthetic environment pitting observational correlation against causal effect, and find it fails instructively. In Simpson's-paradox worlds, where the two have systematically opposite signs, increasing the fraction of interventional samples in pretraining does not improve causal direction: the magnitude of the model's do()-response grows monotonically, yet its sign is copied from the observational context. What governs whether interventional evidence is used is not the training mixture but the evidence type present in the context at inference time. Under an identical training recipe, a purely observational context induces systematic sign reversal in 29/50 worlds, a mixed context in 19/50, while aligned interventional probes alone yield 41/50 correct. Erasing observational evidence from the context immediately releases the suppressed causal interpolation ability (ratio_true = +0.56); a four-state content manipulation shows the switch is content-mediated and graded. The suppression is stable across training seeds (11/11 strong reversals persist on a matched-protocol second seed) and robust as a rate at 0.93B parameters (31.8% vs. 6% reversals in the matched probe-only arm), even as absolute gains shrink four-fold. An external audit on CLadder exposes a learned positive-effect prior with a two-layer structure: sign-randomized retraining removes it in-distribution but not out-of-distribution. We summarize: the capability lives in the weights; the switch lives in the context, and activation patching localizes the switch to the middle layers' observational rows. We further quantify the sampling noise floor of probe-based causal evaluation and an evidence-averaging protocol that cuts sign errors from 26% to 9%.

</details>


### [108] [ARB: A Matched Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark Dataset for AI-Text Detector Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29539)
*Gaetano Perrone,Simon Pietro Romano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB)，一个包含四种文本变体（人类写作、直接LLM生成、LLM改写人类文本、同模型改写LLM文本）的基准数据集，基于1800个人类源文本和四个开源生成器构建。实验发现，在传统benchmark上表现良好的AI文本检测器（FastDetectGPT和Binoculars-falcon-7b）在检测LLM改写的人类文本时性能急剧下降（召回率从90%以上跌至15-31%），而检测LLM改写LLM文本时下降较小。结果表明常规基准的性能不能预测检测器在人类文本被LLM改写场景下的实际表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测基准仅比较人类文本与LLM直接生成的文本，然而实际场景中用户常使用LLM改写人类内容。虽然已有研究证明改写会降低检测器性能，但常规基准的评测结果能否预测改写场景下的检测行为仍然未知。

Method: 构建ARB基准，包含1800个人类源文本（来自XSum、WritingPrompts和OpenWebText各600篇），使用四个开源生成器（Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B）生成四种匹配变体：HUMAN（人类写作）、Free-LLM（直接生成）、H2L（LLM改写人类文本）、LLM2L（同模型改写LLM文本）。在严格的1%假阳性率下评估五种检测器（FastDetectGPT, Binoculars-falcon-7b, RADAR, BERT-Defense, RoBERTa-Defense）的召回率。

Result: FastDetectGPT和Binoculars-falcon-7b对直接生成文本的检测率分别为91.2%和93.5%，但对LLM改写的人类文本检测率骤降至30.8%和15.1%（下降60-78个百分点）；对同模型改写的LLM文本仍保持78.3%和83.0%的召回率（仅下降10-13个百分点）。RADAR表现类似（66.8%降至12.2%），而BERT-Defense和RoBERTa-Defense在所有场景下召回率均低于3%。

Conclusion: 常规human-vs-LLM基准上的检测器性能不能迁移到LLM改写的人类文本场景。检测器对LLM改写LLM文本仍然较为稳健，但对LLM改写人类文本的检测效果极差，说明需要专门针对该场景开发新的检测方法或基准。

Abstract: Standard AI-text detection benchmarks compare human-written text against text generated directly by large language models (LLMs). While prior work has shown that rewriting and paraphrasing can degrade detector performance, it remains unclear whether performance measured on this conventional benchmark predicts detector behavior when human-authored content is rewritten by an LLM. To address this gap, we introduce Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB), built from 1,800 human source texts (600 each from XSum, WritingPrompts, and OpenWebText) and four open-weight generators (Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B). Each source item yields four matched variants: human-written (HUMAN), direct LLM generation (Free-LLM), LLM-rewritten human text (H2L), and same-generator LLM-rewritten LLM text (LLM2L). We evaluated five detectors (FastDetectGPT, Binoculars-falcon-7b, RADAR, BERT-Defense, RoBERTa-Defense) at a strict 1%-false-positive operating point (TPR@1%FPR). FastDetectGPT and Binoculars-falcon-7b detected 91.2% and 93.5\% of direct LLM text, but only 30.8% and 15.1% of human text an LLM had rewritten, a drop of 60-78 percentage points. The same detectors retained 78.3% and 83.0% recall when LLM text was rewritten by the same model, a much smaller decline of 10-13 points. RADAR followed the same pattern (66.8% to 12.2%), while BERT-Defense and RoBERTa-Defense stayed below 3% recall across all regimes. These results show that detector performance measured on the conventional human-vs-LLM benchmark does not transfer to human-authored text revised by an LLM, even though the same detectors remain largely robust to LLM-only rewriting.

</details>


### [109] [Sycophancy Undermines Epistemic Vigilance in Cooperative Vision-Language Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29585)
*Rupak Sarkar,Neha Srikanth,Saloni Gupta,Claire Bonial,Philip Resnik,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个信息不对称的对话式"找不同"任务来测量视觉-语言模型在合作场景中的认知警觉性，发现模型经常忽略自己私有图像中的关键证据而倾向于同意对话伙伴，并将这种行为与"谄媚"（sycophancy）联系起来，最后证明用任务无关的谄媚示例学习到的向量进行模型引导可以有效减少认知警觉相关错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在合作对话中会不断更新信念并对新信息保持认知警觉，当新信息与已有信念冲突时能够发现并修复不一致。为了让AI系统在复杂合作任务中成为可靠伙伴，它们也需要具备类似能力——即权衡新信息与自己私有证据及共享语境，并在出现不一致时适当指出。然而现有研究缺乏对这一能力的系统性测量。

Method: 提出了一个信息不对称的对话式"找不同"任务：两个模型各自私密地看到一张图片，需要通过对话判断两张图片是否相同，若不同则找出差异。通过这种设置测量模型在面对伙伴信息与自己私有证据冲突时的认知警觉行为，并使用从任务无关谄媚示例中学到的向量对模型进行引导以减少谄媚行为。

Result: 模型在此任务上经常失败：它们倾向于忽略自己私有图像中的关键证据去赞同对话伙伴，即使这种认同是没有根据的。这种认知警觉的违反与谄媚行为相关，表现为合作目标导向对话中的过度迁就和薄弱的证据支撑。通过向量引导减少谄媚后，模型的认知警觉相关错误显著降低，使其更忠实地报告证据。

Conclusion: 认知警觉性的缺失是视觉-语言模型在信息不对称合作任务中不可靠的重要原因，其本质与谄媚行为相关。通过任务无关的谄媚向量引导可以有效提升模型的认知警觉性和报告忠实度，从而使模型成为更可靠的信息不对称合作伙伴。

Abstract: To maintain common ground in cooperative conversation, humans iteratively update their beliefs as conversation participants share new information; participants who are epistemically vigilant detect when new information conflicts with prior beliefs and take steps to repair these conflicts. In order for AI systems to serve as reliable partners in complex cooperative tasks, they must similarly weigh incoming information against their own private evidence and shared context and appropriately surface inconsistencies when they arise. To measure the epistemic vigilance of vision-language models in cooperative settings, we present an information-asymmetric, dialog-based "spot-the-difference" task. Two models are privately shown one image each, and must determine through conversation whether the images are identical or, if not, identify the difference. Models routinely fail at this: they frequently overlook key evidence in their private image in favor of agreeing with their conversational partner, even when their agreement is unwarranted. We relate these violations of epistemic vigilance to the broader behavior of sycophancy, which manifests itself in cooperative goal-oriented dialog as over-accommodation and weak evidential grounding. Our results show that model steering to reduce sycophancy with a vector learned from task-agnostic sycophancy examples can reduce epistemic vigilance-related errors, making models more faithful reporters of their evidence, and in turn, more reliable partners in information-asymmetric cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [110] [ResKV: Reconstructing Omitted Attention Contributions for Fixed-Budget KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29591)
*Yuhang Zhan,Lisi Chen,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ResKV将固定KV预算分为精确主缓存和紧凑残差缓存，通过重建被驱逐token在softmax注意力中的分子和分母贡献，在不扰动主缓存token的前提下提升压缩质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存驱逐方法永久丢弃未选中的token，导致其在注意力中的聚合贡献完全丢失；而合并类方法虽然保留更多信息，但可能扰动本应保持精确的键和值。因此需要在有限预算下同时兼顾信息保留和精确性。

Method: ResKV将固定KV预算划分为精确主缓存和紧凑残差缓存。残差缓存重建被驱逐token在softmax注意力中的贡献（包括分子和分母）；主缓存token和残差条目参与同一softmax归一化，恢复注意力分子和分母质量而非事后校正。构建时通过验证代理确定每层和每个KV头的残差分配，解码时通过动态门控调整每个查询的残差贡献。

Result: 在LongBench和RULER上，覆盖query-aware和query-agnostic设置、多种骨干网络、缓存预算和代表性压缩基线，ResKV在相同的保留KV预算下展现出广泛改进，同时保持了压缩解码的实际效率（包括峰值内存使用和长上下文解码吞吐量）。

Conclusion: 通过将省略token的信息量化为softmax注意力分子和分母中的残差统计量，ResKV有效弥合了精确缓存与信息保留之间的鸿沟，在压缩效率和推理性能之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: KV cache compression is essential for efficient long-context inference. Existing eviction methods permanently discard unselected tokens and consequently remove their aggregate contribution to attention. Merging-based alternatives preserve more information but can perturb retained keys and values that should remain exact. We observe that the information omitted by cache eviction can be formulated as residual statistics in both the numerator and denominator of softmax attention. Based on this observation, we propose ResKV, which divides a fixed KV budget into an exact main cache and a compact residual cache that reconstructs the contribution of omitted tokens. ResKV lets main-cache tokens and residual entries participate in the same softmax normalization, so residual entries restore both attention numerator and denominator mass rather than acting as a post-hoc correction. A construction-time validation proxy determines residual allocation for each layer and KV head, while a decode-time dynamic gate adjusts residual contributions for individual queries. Comprehensive evaluations on LongBench and RULER, covering query-aware and query-agnostic settings, multiple backbones, cache budgets, and representative compression baselines, demonstrate broad improvements under the same retained KV budget while preserving the practical efficiency of compressed decoding, including peak memory usage and long-context decode throughput.

</details>


### [111] [FriendBench: Benchmarking Dyadic Familiarity Inference in Humans and Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29602)
*Jeffrey M. Girard,Jason Z. Zheng,Jacqueline R. Vertino,Antony D'Avirro,Benjamin Peloquin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了FriendBench基准，用于从20秒双人破冰对话片段中判断两人是熟人还是陌生人。通过对比26个模型与人类在文本、音频、视频三种模态上的表现，发现最佳模型与人类在准确率上无显著差异，但模型的判断偏向"陌生人"，与人类保持两类答案平衡不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交场景的理解往往依赖于行为而非单纯的言语，但目前缺乏系统性的基准来评估AI模型在非语言社交线索推断上的能力。作者希望探究AI模型是否能像人类一样，仅通过简短互动片段准确判断两个人的熟悉程度。

Method: 构建96个平衡的双人破冰对话不同二元组，所有对话使用相同类型的提示词，确保只有互动方式本身能揭示答案。从7家公司的26个模型，分别在文本、音频和视频三种模态下进行测试，并与匹配的人类评审小组进行对比。

Result: 最佳模型与人类评审在三种模态下的准确率上统计上不可区分，但达到结果的方式不同：人类在两类答案上保持平衡，而最强的模型倾向于回答"陌生人"，这反映的是有效先验的差异而非判别能力差异。更丰富的通道对两者的帮助不均等，只有人类能从可见行为中获益。

Conclusion: 模型虽然能在准确率上达到人类水平，但在判断倾向（先验）上与人类存在系统性差异，且对视觉行为信息的利用不如人类有效。研究公开了所有刺激材料、人类评分和模型预测，以促进该领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: Reading a social situation often depends on behavior, not words alone. We introduce FriendBench, a benchmark for inferring whether two people are already familiar or are meeting as strangers, from a 20-second clip of a dyadic ice-breaker conversation. Every pair answers the same type of prompt, so only the manner of interaction can reveal the answer. Across text, audio, and video, we compare 26 models from seven companies against matched human panels over 96 balanced dyads. The best model and the human crowd are statistically indistinguishable on accuracy in every modality, but reach it differently: humans stay balanced across the two answers, while the strongest models lean toward "stranger"---a difference in effective prior, not discrimination. Richer channels help both unequally, and only humans gain from visible behavior on top of speech. We release the stimuli, human ratings, and model predictions.

</details>


### [112] [Evolving language compositionality in a frequency-structured meaning space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29642)
*Fabio De Ponte,Eloise Gaines-White,Conor Houghton,Seth Bullock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过迭代学习模型研究了语言演化中频率分布如何影响组合性结构的形成，发现高频意义可逃避语法约束（与自然语言一致），但频率施加在部分而非整体意义向量上时，语言无法跨代传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前迭代学习模型发现语言组合性可因重复传播和"学习瓶颈"自发产生，但频率因素如何影响组合性的特征尚未被系统探索。

Method: 在迭代学习模型框架下，设计了两种频率结构条件：一种是将频率施加于整体意义向量，另一种是将频率施加于意义的组成部分；观察语言在代际间传播时的组合性演化与稳定性。

Result: 高频意义可以逃避低频意义所遵循的语法压力（与自然语言观察一致）；但当频率施加在部分而非整体意义向量上时，尽管高频元素能被可靠习得，语言仍无法跨代传播。

Conclusion: 频率只有在作用于学习者能够整体习得的形式-意义单元时，才能塑造新兴的语言结构；当频率分布在更小的单元上时，无法支撑组合性泛化所需的关联结构，从而阻碍稳定的语言传播。

Abstract: The iterated learning model was introduced to investigate language evolution: the way in which the characteristic properties of human languages have been shaped, at least partly, by repeated transmission from one language user to another. The key finding is that language compositionality can arise spontaneously as a consequence of language being passed repeatedly through a language learning bottleneck. Here we explore how changing the frequency of different meanings, so that some meanings occur much more frequently than others, affects the character of its compositionality. We find that, as observed in natural languages, high-frequency meanings can escape the pressure to conform to the grammar that characterizes lower-frequency meanings. However, when the frequency structure is instead imposed on parts rather than on whole meaning vectors, the language fails to transmit across generations. This occurs despite the fact that the most frequent elements are reliably learned. These results suggest that frequency can shape emergent linguistic structure only when the frequency distribution is defined over form-meaning units that learners can acquire holistically. When frequency is instead distributed over smaller units, it fails to support the relational structure required for compositional generalisation, thereby preventing stable language transmission.

</details>


### [113] [TokTier: Exact Stateful Tokenization for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29678)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TokTier是一个有状态的tokenization服务，通过增量修复和GPU加速，在不改变token ID的前提下将LLM推理的tokenization开销大幅降低（增量修复高达437x加速，GPU全量tokenization达491x加速），从而显著提升首token延迟（中位数降低16-34%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务系统通常缓存prompt的KV状态，但前端仍会对每次请求重新tokenize完整文本。这在coding agent场景中尤为严重——短小的追加操作会改变token边界，导致无法复用。实际数据显示，在94.1%的prompt缓存命中率下，tokenization可占首token时间的64%，成为关键瓶颈。

Method: 核心设计是增量tokenization：对session continuation，仅重新tokenize追加附近的窗口，并通过稳定的边界检查确认拼接正确，失败时扩大窗口或回退全量tokenization。对无可复用前缀的调用，将GPT家族的正则预tokenization分解为局部规则，并在GPU上执行精确预tokenization和BPE。此外配备采样影子验证器对在线流量进行复检。

Result: 在17个tokenizer家族、1.5x10^10次分割检查、12.4TB文本语料和93,000+重放agent步骤中零分歧。增量修复耗时0.5-1.1ms（100K至3M字符），比HF快437x，比完全预热的Gigatoken在1M字符时快2.1x。GPU全量tokenization处理1M字符请求仅0.87ms，比HF快491x，比最快CPU方法快23.4x。在vLLM下中位TTFT下降16-34%，P99下降23%。

Conclusion: TokTier证明了有状态tokenization服务的可行性：通过增量窗口修复与GPU加速，它能够在保证token ID与全量参考tokenization完全一致的前提下，将tokenization从LLM推理管道的性能瓶颈中消除，同时显著提升系统的吞吐能力（4个修复核+1个GPU可维持1,821 req/s，而16核无状态前端仅40 req/s）。

Abstract: LLM serving systems cache prompt KV state, yet most front ends still re-tokenize the full request text on every call. The cost lands on coding agents, which resubmit a long transcript after each small tool result, and reuse is hard because even a short append can change token boundaries near the end of the previous sequence. Across 153,951 calls from two agent ecosystems, the median call appends about 1.4K characters, and only 1.0-3.6% of calls start or rebuild a session with contexts of millions of characters. At a 94.1% fleet prompt-cache hit rate, tokenization reaches up to 64% of time to first token.
  TokTier is a stateful tokenization service with one contract: emitted token IDs are always identical to full reference tokenization of the request text. For a session continuation, it re-tokenizes a small window around the append and splices only after a per-request stable-boundary check, widening the window or falling back to full tokenization on failure. For a call without a reusable prefix, it decomposes GPT-family regex pre-tokenization into run-local rules and runs exact pre-tokenization and BPE on a GPU. A sampled shadow verifier re-checks live traffic.
  Across 17 tokenizer families, differential campaigns cover 1.5x10^10 split checks, a 12.4 TB real-text corpus, and 93,000+ replayed agent steps, with zero divergence. Incremental repair takes 0.5-1.1 ms from 100K to 3M characters, up to 437x faster than HF tokenization and 2.1x faster at 1M than the strongest cache-based baseline (Gigatoken) fully prewarmed. GPU full tokenization encodes a 1M-character request in 0.87 ms, up to 491x below HF and 23.4x below the fastest published CPU method. With vLLM, median time to first token drops 16-34% and P99 drops 23% under recorded bursts. Under a 50 ms P99 objective, four repair cores plus one GPU sustain 1,821 requests/s where a 16-core stateless front end saturates at 40.

</details>


### [114] [TokenSwap: Benchmarking and Reducing the Modality Gap in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28640)
*Andong Hua,Colton Bishop,Igor Mordatch,Arian Hosseini,Jindong Gu,Aleksandra Faust,Rebecca Roelofs,Yao Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TokenSwap方法，通过将文本概念替换为语义对齐的图像来构建跨模态等价输入，并基于此构建TokenSwap-Bench基准。在42个多模态大语言模型上发现普遍存在的模态差距（平均19.6%的性能下降），推理模型展现出更小的模态差距，而将TokenSwap纳入训练可有效缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在语义等价的跨模态输入下应产生一致响应，但实际存在系统性预测差异。作者旨在量化并理解这一模态差距，探索其成因及缓解策略。

Method: 提出TokenSwap方法：将文本概念替换为语义对齐的图像，生成视觉token与文本token交错排列的输入序列。基于此将MMLU等文本基准转换为图像交错版本TokenSwap-Bench，并在42个MLLM上评估模态差距。

Result: 42个MLLM上均存在模态差距，性能下降4.2%至47.4%，平均19.6%±3.3%。推理模型差距更小（平均10.1% vs 非推理模型25.5%）。提示策略和扩展训练算力无法可靠缩小差距，但训练中引入TokenSwap可有效缓解且保持原有性能。

Conclusion: 模态差距是多模态大语言模型中普遍存在的系统性缺陷，推理模型天然具有更强的跨模态鲁棒性，而通过TokenSwap进行训练是缩小该差距的有效手段。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) should generate consistent responses given semantically equivalent inputs across modalities. However, we observe a systematic discrepancy in model predictions under such cross-modal variations. Specifically, we define the modality gap as the difference in model performance under semantically equivalent textual and multimodal inputs. We introduce TokenSwap, a method that constructs such inputs by replacing textual concepts with semantically aligned images, resulting in sequences where visual tokens are interleaved with text tokens. Based on TokenSwap, we transform existing text-based benchmarks such as MMLU into image-interleaved counterparts, resulting in TokenSwap-Bench. Across 42 MLLMs, we observe a pervasive modality gap, with performance decreasing by 4.2% to 47.4% when moving from text-only to image-interleaved inputs, averaging 19.6% +/- 3.3% across models. Notably, we observe that reasoning models exhibit consistently smaller gaps, achieving an average gap of 10.1% compared to 25.5% for non-reasoning models. In contrast, neither prompting strategies nor scaling training compute alone reliably reduces the modality gap. Finally, we demonstrate that incorporating TokenSwap during training effectively mitigates this gap while preserving strong text-only and vision-language performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Safety, or Just Capability? A Validity Audit of Agent-Safety Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28685)
*Youting Wang,Xiao Han,Dingyan Shang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统验证了四个智能体安全基准（R-Judge、InjecAgent、AgentHarm、AgentDojo）的有效性，发现基准得分被混用作"智能体安全性"存在严重问题：指标本身有缺陷（随机猜测即可获得高分）、不同基准排序不一致、能力与安全性的关系在小样本下不稳定。研究者呼吁安全声明必须明确标注基准、指标、目标行为和模型集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体安全基准的得分经常被不加区分地引用为智能体的"安全性"，但不同基准测量的是不同的行为，缺乏对这些基准作为测量工具的严格验证。

Method: 对四个安全基准在其官方实现和作者提供的评分器下运行最多22个模型，同时统一协议测量MMLU和GPQA作为能力复合指标。分析指标本身的缺陷（F1评分下的随机策略表现）、基准间排序一致性、能力与安全表现的相关性，并进行留一组织验证和聚类bootstrap分析，扩展至41模型面板验证稳健性。

Result: 发现"总是正向"策略在R-Judge上F1=0.690，超过21个模型中的5个。三个广覆盖基准对同一18个模型排序不同，小样本面板导致该分歧（n=7时相关性-0.64，n=18时+0.02）。能力预测任务成功（ρ=+0.60）但与错位安全负相关（ρ=-0.44）；在更大面板上该负相关减弱至-0.16。AgentHarm与三模板越狱安全有最强关联（ρ=+0.72），但这属于收敛效度而非一般安全性证据。

Conclusion: 安全基准得分不能互换使用；不同基准测量不同行为，存在指标缺陷和小样本假象。任何安全声明都应明确说明所用的基准、指标、目标行为及模型面板，这是最低要求。

Abstract: Agent-safety benchmarks measure different behaviors, and their scores get quoted interchangeably as an agent's safety. We treat four of them (R-Judge, InjecAgent, AgentHarm, AgentDojo) as measurements to be validated, running each under its official implementation and author-provided scorer on up to 22 models, with MMLU and GPQA measured by us under one protocol as a capability composite. The metric is the first problem. On any binary trace-judgment benchmark scored by $F_1$, an ``always positive'' policy attains $F_1 = 2π/(1+π)$; on R-Judge that is $0.690$, above five of the 21 models that actually discriminate. The three broad-coverage benchmarks then rank the same 18 models differently, and the trade-off behind that disagreement is a small-panel artifact: R-Judge specificity against AgentHarm safety correlates $-0.64$ at $n{=}7$ and $+0.02$ at $n{=}18$, and a quarter of random size-7 subsets reach $|ρ| \geq 0.5$ around that near-zero value. Held-out validity turns on which outcome you pick. Capability predicts task success ($ρ{=}{+}0.60$) but correlates negatively with misalignment safety ($ρ{=}{-}0.44$, $n{=}21$). On their paired $n{=}20$ panel, the corresponding contrast is $Δ{=}{-}1.00$ (95% CI $[-1.48, -0.49]$, $p<0.001$), and it survives leave-one-organization-out and organization-clustered bootstrap analyses. On an expanded 41-model panel, the misalignment correlation weakens to $-0.16$ (95% CI $[-0.54, +0.22]$) and jailbreak strengthens to $+0.34$, though neither change is significant. \mbox{AgentHarm} shows the strongest held-out association, $ρ{=}{+}0.72$ with three-template jailbreak safety after controlling capability. But both instruments score harmful compliance, so this is evidence of convergent validity rather than general safety. Naming the benchmark, metric, target behavior, and model panel is the minimum a safety claim needs.

</details>


### [116] [SciToolAgent-Evo: An Ontology-Aware Self-Evolving Agent for Open-World Scientific Tool Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28692)
*Yuqi Tang,Chenyi Zhou,Libin Wang,Keyan Ding,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciToolAgent-Evo，一个面向开放世界科学工具获取的Ontology感知自进化智能体，通过进化记忆、Ontology工具图、LinUCB bandit门控机制实现动态探索与利用平衡，并在新基准OpenSciToolBench（900个任务、四个难度级别）上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体依赖预定义工具空间和静态语义，无法适应开放世界科学工作流中工具需求、能力和边界动态演化的场景，亟需一种能够自主获取和整合新工具的自进化机制。

Method: 1) 进化记忆系统存储技能与经验，从对比轨迹中蒸馏可泛化知识；2) Ontology化的工具图支持新工具语义的在线补全与无缝集成；3) 推理阶段通过LinUCB-based bandit gate动态平衡探索与利用，主动发起工具获取请求。

Result: 在提出的OpenSciToolBench基准（含900个真实任务、四个难度级别）上，SciToolAgent-Evo取得了最先进（SOTA）的性能，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: Ontology感知的自进化机制能够有效解决开放世界科学工具获取问题，为LLM智能体在动态科学工作流中的长期自主演化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have been increasingly adopted in scientific research for organizing and invoking specialized computational tools. However, their reliance on predefined tool spaces with static semantics limits their applicability to open-world scientific workflows, where tool requirements, capabilities, and boundaries evolve dynamically. To this end, we propose SciToolAgent-Evo, an ontology-aware self-evolving agent for open-world scientific tool acquisition. Driven by an evolving memory of skills, experiences, and an ontologized tool graph, it distills generalizable knowledge from contrastive trajectories during accumulation, whereas during inference, it formulates active requests and utilizes a LinUCB-based bandit gate to dynamically balance exploration and exploitation. Once a novel tool is acquired, its scientific ontology is completed online for seamless integration into the known graph. Moreover, we introduce OpenSciToolBench, a benchmark containing 900 realistic tasks across four difficulty levels. Extensive evaluations show that SciToolAgent-Evo achieves state-of-the-art performance, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [117] [Model or Harness? An Interaction-Centric Taxonomy for Localizing Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28802)
*Harsh Raj,Vipul Gupta,Anas Mahmoud,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Darvin Yi,Aakash Sabharwal,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以交互为中心的智能体故障分类体系（taxonomy），将41种故障模式映射到组件间的交互边上，并指明修复责任方（模型侧、脚手架侧或环境/评分侧），解决现有评估中"修复分配问题"（repair-assignment problem）。该分类体系跨不同智能体架构通用，并通过独立推理智能体评估验证其可复现性（最强judge达到Cohen's κ=0.76）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将智能体故障简化为系统级结果，掩盖了故障源头和应采取的干预措施。同一可见失败可能源于模型后训练、harness工程、环境设计或基准修复，而现有故障分类法多为基准特定且缺乏共享结构，难以指导实际改进。

Method: 提出交互中心分类体系：将41种故障模式映射到两个组件之间的边，并标注故障侧（fault side）以指明修复归属。通过公共基准、模型系统卡片、公开报告和日志轨迹中的案例进行验证，并使用独立推理智能体作为judge评估分类的可复现性。

Result: 在四个前沿模型上，最强judge与人类类别标签的一致性达到Cohen's κ=0.76，表明该分类体系捕捉的是共享结构而非标注者特定偏好。

Conclusion: 该交互中心故障分类体系具有可操作性：模型侧故障指向后训练目标，harness侧故障指向脚手架和工具集成修复，环境或评分器故障揭示需重新设计的评估条件，且适用于从编码助手到长期个人助手再到多智能体系统等各类架构。

Abstract: Existing evaluations often reduce agent failures to system-level outcomes, obscuring where the fault originated and which intervention would improve the agent system. This creates a repair-assignment problem: the same visible failure may call for model post-training, harness engineering, environment redesign, or benchmark repair depending on its source. Because agent behavior emerges from interactions among models, harnesses, users, tools, memory, and environments, outcome-level labels are often insufficient for improvement. Most failure taxonomies do little to resolve this problem because they are benchmark-specific and lack a shared structure. We introduce an interaction-centric taxonomy that localizes failures to the interactions in which they originate and identifies the responsible component. It organizes 41 failure modes by assigning each to an edge between two components and a fault side indicating where the repair belongs. This makes the taxonomy actionable: model-side failures identify targets for post-training, harness-side failures point to scaffolding and tool-integration fixes, and environment or grader failures reveal evaluation conditions requiring redesign. The schema applies across agent architectures, from coding assistants to long-horizon personal assistants and multi-agent systems. We ground the taxonomy in worked examples from public benchmarks, model system cards, published reports, and logged agent trajectories, and evaluate its reproducibility using independent reasoning agents as judges. Across four frontier models, the strongest judge reaches Cohen's $κ=0.76$ against human category labels, suggesting that the categories capture shared structure rather than annotator-specific preferences.

</details>


### [118] [Best Friends, Not Forever: Evaluating Long-Horizon Persona Collapse and Behavioral Drift in AI Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28818)
*Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Akshara Prabhakar,Yu Li,Daniel Lee,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ANCHOR，一个受控合成审计框架，用于独立测量AI伴侣的"人格坍塌"（失去部署的角色、边界、价值观或风格）和"行为漂移"（这些属性的渐进或反复侵蚀）。研究涵盖2,008段对话、27个人格、9种交互安排、3种记忆设置和4个评估模型，结果显示没有任何模型和配置能可靠地保持人格一致性和轨迹记忆——轨迹准确率平均仅44.4%，用户状态回忆接近四选一随机水平，表明当前系统无法可靠支持长期伴侣连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然AI伴侣可以给出局部可接受的回复，但用户可能依赖稳定的角色和共享历史，而局部合适的回复并不能保证角色一致性和历史记忆的持久性。现有研究缺乏对"人格坍塌"和"行为漂移"这两个长期失败模式的系统性可观测测量方法。

Method: 提出ANCHOR审计框架：(1) 身份探针（Identity Probe），使用密封的102项问卷与回合级判断来测量人格执行；(2) 轨迹探针（Trajectory Probe），从35个对话库中评分110个校准的反事实问题来测量轨迹回忆。研究覆盖2,008段对话、27个人格、9种交互安排、3种记忆设置和4个模型。

Result: 没有任何被评估的模型和配置能可靠地同时保持人格一致性和轨迹回忆：轨迹准确率平均仅44.4%，用户状态回忆接近四选一随机水平；没有一种上下文条件或记忆设置能一致解决这些失败。问卷保留率因模型和人格方面而异，与回合级行为不一致，且对评估者选择敏感。

Conclusion: 当前AI伴侣系统尚不能可靠支持长期陪伴连续性。审计必须区分人格执行、轨迹回忆、评估者来源和部署上下文，而非将它们合并为单一的信任或稳定性分数；人格一致性评估需要多维度的分离测量方法。

Abstract: As AI companions increasingly mediate repeated social interaction, users may rely on a stable role and shared history, yet locally acceptable replies do not ensure that either persists. We study two observable long-horizon failures: 'persona collapse', the loss of a deployed role, boundaries, values, or style, and 'behavioral drift', the gradual or recurrent erosion of those properties. We introduce ANCHOR, a controlled synthetic audit that separately measures persona enactment and trajectory recall. The study contains 2,008 conversations spanning 27 personas, nine interaction schedules, three generated memory settings, and four evaluated models. The Identity Probe combines a sealed 102-item questionnaire with turn-level judgments, while the Trajectory Probe scores 110 calibrated counterfactual questions from 35 conversation banks. Our results show that no evaluated model and configuration reliably preserves either dimensions: trajectory accuracy averages only 44.4%, user-state recall remains near four-option chance, and no tested context condition or memory consistently resolves these failures. Questionnaire retention also varies by model and persona facet, disagrees with turn-level behavior, and is sensitive to evaluator choice. These results indicate that current systems do not yet reliably support long-horizon companion continuity and that audits must distinguish persona enactment, trajectory recall, evaluator provenance, and deployment context rather than collapse them into a single trust or stability score.

</details>


### [119] [Fragility of Value under Imperfect Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28881)
*Winter Cross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个对齐问题的数学模型，研究了在理想化对齐训练后，代理的价值函数虽满足代理条件但仍可能导致灾难性后果的条件，并发现过度优化是危险的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统承担更多责任，确保其与人类价值观对齐变得至关重要。人们担心人类价值观是脆弱的，即过度优化一个不完美的代理价值函数可能导致灾难性结果。本文旨在通过形式化模型来理解这种脆弱性发生的条件。

Method: 作者构建了一个对齐问题的数学模型，其中代理经历理想化的对齐训练，保证其价值函数在优化世界之前满足某个代理条件。通过数学分析，识别了人类价值函数和各种代理条件精度下，什么样的代理会被部署（即具有η-灾难性价值函数，保证在优化能力极限下将人类价值期望降至η以下）。

Result: 研究确定了人类价值函数和代理条件精度的条件下，η-灾难性价值函数的代理被部署的充分条件，凸显了过度优化的危险性。

Conclusion: 结果警示了过度优化的危害，并支持了限制优化压力的AI设计（如quantilizers），而不是仅依赖部署前的训练。

Abstract: As more responsibility is placed upon AI systems, it becomes increasingly important to guarantee that these systems are aligned with humanity. A common fear in AI safety is that human value is fragile -- that is, optimizing too heavily for an imperfect proxy to human values will lead to a catastrophic outcome. In this paper, we present a model of the alignment problem where an agent undergoes idealized alignment training that guarantees its value function satisfies a proxy condition before optimizing the world. Our primary results identify conditions on the human value function and the accuracy of several proxy conditions under which an agent with an $η$-catastrophic value function, one that is guaranteed to take the expectation of human value below $η$ in the limit of optimizing power, would be deployed. Our results highlight the danger of overoptimization and motivate AI designs that limit optimization pressure, such as quantilizers, rather than relying solely on pre-deployment training.

</details>


### [120] [Identifying Informative Environments for Cognition Parameter Inference via Bayesian Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28894)
*Manisha Dubey,Rimvydas Rubavicius,N. Siddharth,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将认知规划实验设计形式化为贝叶斯实验设计（BED）问题，将实验环境作为设计变量，并引入摊销贝叶斯实验设计框架，在Mouselab-MDP范式上验证其与精确蒙特卡洛BED的环境排序高度一致，同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算认知建模中，贝叶斯逆规划的参数推断效果严重依赖实验环境，但现有方法通常将环境视为固定不变的，没有回答"哪些认知实验环境对认知参数推断最具信息量"这一关键问题。

Method: 将认知规划实验的环境设计形式化为贝叶斯实验设计（BED）问题，将实验环境作为设计变量；建立一个精确的蒙特卡洛BED基准，并引入摊销贝叶斯实验设计框架用于高效的后验推断和设计评估；在Mouselab-MDP过程追踪范式上进行实验验证。

Result: 在Mouselab-MDP范式上，摊销BED与精确蒙特卡洛BED在环境排序上高度一致，但计算成本大幅降低；研究还发现不存在单一环境对所有认知推断目标都最优，揭示了预期信息增益、后验可恢复性和信息效率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为设计信息丰富的认知实验以支持贝叶斯参数推断提供了原则性的框架，揭示了不同认知推断目标下实验环境设计的权衡关系。

Abstract: Computational cognitive modeling seeks to infer latent cognitive mechanisms underlying observed behavior. Bayesian inverse planning provides a principled framework for such inference, but its success depends critically on the experimental environment. Existing approaches typically treat environments as fixed, leaving open the question of which cognitive experiments are most informative for cognition parameter inference. We formulate the design of cognitive planning experiments as a Bayesian Experimental Design (BED) problem, treating the experimental environment as the design variable. We establish an exact Monte Carlo BED benchmark and introduce an amortized Bayesian experimental design framework for efficient posterior inference and design evaluation. Experiments on the Mouselab-MDP process-tracing paradigm show that amortized BED closely matches the environment rankings of exact Monte Carlo BED while substantially reducing computational cost. We further show that no single environment is uniformly optimal across cognitive inference objectives, revealing trade-offs between expected information gain, posterior recoverability, and information efficiency. These results provide a principled framework for designing informative cognitive experiments for Bayesian parameter inference.

</details>


### [121] [NeSyFS: A Neuro-symbolic Fast-Slow Thinking Framework for LLM Agent under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28942)
*Duo Xu,Faramarz Fekri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号快慢思考（NeSyFS）框架，用于解决LLM智能体在部分可观测环境下的决策问题，通过知识图谱表示信念状态、快速思考模块执行反应式动作、慢速思考模块基于扭曲顺序蒙特卡洛（TSMC）算法进行不确定性规划，并在三个基准数据集上取得了显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自主智能体在部分可观测环境中面临信念状态推断、任务目标错位和不确定性规划三大挑战，而现有方法基于完整或摘要化动作-观测历史进行决策，其中的冗余和无关信息会误导LLM智能体的决策。

Method: 提出NeSyFS框架，包含三个核心模块：1) 用知识图谱（KG）表示信念状态并为各模块提供三元组上下文；2) 快速思考模块执行反应式动作；3) 慢速思考模块遵循扭曲顺序蒙特卡洛（TSMC）算法的高层结构进行不确定性感知规划；4) 反思模块用于回馈快速思考动作并纠正任务目标错位，在反应式动作反复失败时切换到慢速思考模块。

Result: 在ALFWorld、Webshop和ScienceWorld三个代表性基准上的实验表明，该方法相比先前方法具有显著优势。

Conclusion: NeSyFS框架通过统一的神经符号快慢思考机制有效解决了LLM智能体在部分可观测环境中的核心挑战，验证了其相较于现有方法的优越性。

Abstract: Recently Large Language Models (LLMs) have been increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in applications such as self-reflection, retrieval-augmented generation, and scientific discovery. In these settings, agents must act based on limited observations rather than full environmental states, leading to partial observability. This introduces several key challenges: belief state inference, task objective misalignment, and planning under uncertainty. Prior approaches typically condition actions on full or summarized action-observation histories whose redundant and irrelevant information can mislead the decision making of LLM agent. Inspired by human cognition, we propose a novel neuro-symbolic fast-slow thinking (NeSyFS) framework for LLM agent, addressing the challenges introduced by partial observability in a unified approach. We use a knowledge graph (KG) to represent the belief state, providing triplets as context for every module of NeSyFS. The fast-thinking module performs reactive action, while slow-thinking conducts a new uncertainty-aware planning by following the high-level structure of twisted sequential Monte Carlo (TSMC) algorithm. To mitigate the misalignment of task objective, a reflection module is used to reflect fast-thinking actions, and also switches to the slow-thinking module whenever reactive actions repeatedly fail. Experiments on three representative benchmarks, i.e. ALFWorld, Webshop, and ScienceWorld, demonstrate significant advantages over previous methods.

</details>


### [122] [MerchantBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Long-Term Coherence in E-Commerce Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28956)
*Qiming Shi,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Zhaolu Kang,Yibo Dou,Jiawen Zhu,Tianjun Pan,Shaokang Fu,Chengyu Wang,Siyue Li,Yaping Cheng,Di Weng,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MerchantBench，一个基于真实电商数据构建的365天订单级模拟基准，用于评估大语言模型智能体在长期连贯性（Long-Term Coherence）方面的能力。结果显示，最佳LLM配置仅达到人类参与者平均最终净资产的27.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体基准大多聚焦于有明确成功标准的有限任务，而真实部署场景需要智能体在长时间跨度内保持目标导向的行为，并基于累积证据动态调整决策。缺乏能评估这种长期连贯性的基准环境。

Method: 构建MerchantBench，一个基于98,843条真实电商产品记录、配备26个工具的365天订单级模拟环境。模拟涵盖产品采购、列表与定价控制、现金流管理及混合延迟反馈适应等决策维度，耦合即时上游供应商事件与延迟下游订单结果，要求智能体跟踪订单生命周期并回溯先前决策。对8个LLM在两种智能体框架下进行48次运行（每次365模拟天）的评估。

Result: 最佳LLM配置仅达到人类参与者平均最终净资产的27.3%，揭示了最新LLM与人类在长期决策连贯性和累积效果管理方面存在显著差距。

Conclusion: MerchantBench有效暴露了LLM智能体在长期连贯性上的不足，为未来开发具备长期目标保持和自适应决策能力的LLM智能体提供了可复现的评估基准。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly evaluated as autonomous tool users, yet most benchmarks focus on bounded tasks with immediate success criteria. Real-world deployments often require Long-Term Coherence, the capacity to preserve purposeful behavior across extended horizons while adapting decisions to accumulated evidence. Evaluating this capacity requires a persistent environment in which actions constrain future choices, feedback arrives at heterogeneous delays, and incoherent behavior produces measurable cumulative effects. Seller-side e-commerce provides a suitable setting for this evaluation through recurrent and interdependent decisions over Product Sourcing, Listing and Pricing Control, Cash-Flow Management, and Mixed-Latency Feedback Adaptation. We introduce MerchantBench, a 365-day order-level simulation grounded in 98,843 real e-commerce product records and equipped with 26 tools for agent interaction. MerchantBench couples promptly observable Upstream Supplier Events with delayed Downstream Order Outcomes, requiring agents to follow individual order lifecycles and revisit earlier decisions. We evaluate eight LLMs under two agent frameworks in 48 runs, each spanning 365 simulated days. Our results reveal a substantial gap between even the latest LLMs and human participants, with the best LLM configuration attaining only 27.3\% of the mean final net assets achieved by human participants.

</details>


### [123] [Scaling Scientific Discovery Environments for Turn-Level Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28990)
*Yucheng Xu,Keyi Zhang,Yuyang Yu,Min Zhang,Shiyuan Meng,Pei Chu,Zhongying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciDisco框架，通过构建过程可验证的科学数据环境（SciThèque）、DAG约束的轨迹合成和DiscoPO策略优化方法，训练科学发现智能体，14B参数模型达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在数据驱动的科学发现任务中展现出潜力，但长周期科学分析受限于缺乏对真实科学数据的过程监督环境，导致无法在交互过程中对分析进度进行有效检查和反馈。

Method: 1) 构建SciThèque环境：编译假设、数据集、隐藏证据图和验证器为任务环境，支持交互过程中检查分析进度；2) 提出DAG约束的轨迹合成方法，利用环境生成经过验证器过滤的多轮演示；3) 提出DiscoPO优化方法，利用环境作为训练信号来源，为产生可验证分析证据的动作分配回合级（turn-level）信用。

Result: SciDisco-14B在假设驱动的科学数据分析基准上取得了最先进（SOTA）的结果。

Conclusion: 通过过程可验证环境和回合级信用分配的训练方法，可以有效提升科学发现智能体在长周期数据分析任务上的表现，证明了过程监督在科学数据分析中的价值。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown promising capabilities in data-driven scientific discovery tasks, where an agent interacts with an execution environment and produces a statistical claim. Long-horizon scientific analysis remains constrained by the lack of process supervised environments over real-world scientific data. This paper introduces SciDisco, a scalable framework for training Scientific Discovery agents in process-verifiable environments. SciThèque compiles hypotheses, datasets, hidden evidence graphs, and verifiers into task environments where analytical progress can be checked during interaction. DAG-grounded trajectory synthesis uses these environments to construct verifier-filtered multi-turn demonstrations. DiscoPO then uses the environment as the source of training signal, assigning turn-level credit to actions that produce verifiable analytical evidence. Experiments show that SciDisco-14B reaches state-of-the-art on hypothesis-driven scientific data analysis benchmarks.

</details>


### [124] [MMShopBench: A Real-Log Benchmark for Multimodal, Multi-Turn Shopping Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29002)
*Zeying Hao,Hao Guo,Mengtao Xu,Yimin Hu,Yuheng Song,Zesheng Zhou,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MMShopBench，这是首个基于真实购物日志的多模态、多轮对话购物智能体基准。该基准包含人工标注的购买意图和强制产品需求，要求智能体从用户图像和多轮对话中联合推断需求，并通过图像与文本搜索检索候选产品，最终验证产品是否满足所有需求。通过离线购物沙箱和配套训练集微调开源模型，大幅缩小了与专有模型的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有购物基准大多依赖纯文本或合成请求，未能充分反映现实世界中用户通过图像和语言联合表达复杂购物需求的场景，缺乏能够真实评估多模态多轮购物智能体的基准。

Method: 构建MMShopBench基准：从真实购物日志中清洗和人工标注得到购买意图及强制产品需求的ground-truth；采用证据驱动的多模态评估协议；构建离线购物沙箱保证可复现实验；设计配套训练集用于微调开源模型。

Result: 在MMShopBench上评估了代表性开源和专有模型，微调后的开源模型显著缩小了与领先专有模型之间的性能差距，验证了训练数据的有效性。

Conclusion: MMShopBench填补了真实多模态多轮购物智能体评估的空白，其训练集和离线沙箱为提升开源购物智能体性能提供了有效途径，展示了利用真实购物日志数据微调开源模型的可行性。

Abstract: Online shoppers increasingly turn to AI shopping assistants, using images and multi-turn dialogue to express and refine product needs that are difficult to articulate in text alone. However, existing benchmarks largely rely on text-only or synthetic requests, underrepresenting complex real-world shopping requirements jointly expressed through images and language. We introduce MMShopBench, the first real-log benchmark for multimodal, multi-turn shopping agents. Built from carefully cleaned and manually annotated shopping logs, MMShopBench provides ground-truth annotations of each request's purchase intent and mandatory product requirements. Agents must infer these requirements jointly from user images and multi-turn dialogue, retrieve candidate products through image and text search, and verify that each candidate satisfies all requirements using its product images and structured attributes. We evaluate representative open-source and proprietary models using an evidence-grounded multimodal protocol and construct a companion training set for fine-tuning an open-source model. To ensure reproducible experimentation, we build an offline shopping sandbox, where fine-tuning substantially narrows the performance gap between our open-source model and leading proprietary models, demonstrating the effectiveness of our training data.

</details>


### [125] [Evidence-Grounded Constraint Checking in Construction Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29058)
*Rashid Mushkani,Hugo Berard,Shin Koseki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于证据的专业文档审阅流水线，将提取的事实规范化、确定性执行四状态规则、保留源片段并升级未解决案例。在160个参考任务上评估PDF证据分配器，发现将四图像预算从页面概览改为一个概览加三个重叠瓦片，在项目族标准化决策准确率上提升10.6个百分点，但这种效果在更广泛的测试中不持久，存在分辨率-广度权衡而非通用优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业文档审阅是一个约束检查问题，决策依赖于文本、几何、页面和文档修订之间的关系。现有方法缺乏对证据分配策略的系统评估，特别是页面级概览与区域级瓦片之间的权衡尚未明确。

Method: 采用证据驱动的流水线：规范化提取事实，确定性执行四状态规则，保留源文本片段，对未解决案例进行升级处理。评估设计包含两个部分：重复四系统测试（160个参考任务，来自29个建设项目）和不重叠的两系统广度扩展测试。使用项目簇自助法计算置信区间和精确p值。

Result: 重复测试中，将四图像预算从检索页面概览改为一个概览加三个重叠瓦片，项目族标准化决策准确率提升10.6个百分点（95% CI: 4.3-18.0; p=0.031）。但在更广泛的阻断测试中效果不持久：Region-RAG改变准确率-4.1个百分点（p=0.209），等量图像敏感性偏向页面广度。精确发现集恢复率仍然很低，假通过仍然常见，重复运行一致性校准不佳。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了分辨率-广度权衡而非区域聚焦证据的普适优势，表明需要规则感知的证据路由和专家复核来优化专业文档审阅系统。

Abstract: Professional-document review is a constraint-checking problem in which decisions depend on relations among text, geometry, pages, and document revisions. We present an evidence-grounded pipeline that normalizes extracted facts, executes four-state rules deterministically, retains source spans, and escalates unresolved cases. We evaluate its PDF evidence allocator on 160 reference-based tasks from 29 construction projects using a repeated four-system test and a disjoint two-system breadth extension. In the repeated test, reallocating a four-image budget from retrieved page overviews to one overview and three overlapping tiles improves project-family standardized decision accuracy by 10.6 percentage points (95% project-cluster bootstrap CI: 4.3 to 18.0; exact p = 0.031). This effect does not persist in the broader block: Region-RAG changes accuracy by -4.1 points (95% CI: -10.2 to 1.9; exact p = 0.209), while an equal-image sensitivity favors page breadth. Exact finding-set recovery remains low, false passes remain common, and repeated-run agreement is poorly calibrated. The results identify a resolution-breadth trade-off rather than a universal advantage for region-focused evidence, motivating rule-aware evidence routing and expert review.

</details>


### [126] [On the Generalization of Steering Vectors for Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29062)
*Matthew Nguyen,Kyle Cox,Austin Meek,Iván Arcuschin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了激活引导（activation steering）方法能否提升思维链（CoT）推理的忠实性，即在三个模型上测试引导对不同线索类型、数据集和向量构建方法的泛化能力，发现引导仅在最大模型上稳定有效，且效果主要由评估设置而非训练设置决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链推理对AI安全有重要价值，因为模型口述推理过程可以被监控。但模型有时不会口头表达关键的推理步骤，导致思维链不忠实。作者希望探索激活引导方法是否能改善这种不忠实性，并系统评估其泛化能力。

Method: 在三个模型（Gemma-3 4B, Qwen-3.5 9B, Gemma-3 12B）上，使用四种种构建引导向量的方法（包括一种优化目标不涉及特定线索的方法），在带线索的问答设置中测试激活引导对思维链忠实性的影响，并进行跨线索类型、跨数据集和跨向量构建方法的系统性分析，同时检验引导是否真正促进线索使用而非仅影响口头表达。

Result: 引导仅对最大的模型（Gemma-3 12B）稳定地提高了线索承认；在引导有效时，其效果可广泛泛化到不同线索类型和数据集，效果大小主要由评估设置决定而非向量训练设置；四种向量构建方法效果相似；引导未改变线索使用率，但减少了未承认的隐藏线索使用。

Conclusion: 激活引导对思维链忠实性的提升在模型间存在差异，但在有效的场景中泛化性强。引导并非通过提高线索显著性来改变实际线索使用行为，而是针对性地减少了隐藏线索使用，表明其作用机制更接近改善言语化行为而非影响推理过程。

Abstract: Model capabilities have improved in large part due to scaling chain of thought. This has been a promising development for AI safety--where models verbalize their reasoning, it is possible to monitor it. However, in some cases, models do not verbalize important steps in their reasoning process. For example, models prompted with a cue suggesting the incorrect answer may fail to acknowledge that cue, even when it appears instrumental to their conclusion. When chain of thought (CoT) fails to disclose instrumental reasoning steps, we describe it as unfaithful. Prior work has shown that activation steering can be a useful method to improve faithfulness in CoT. We extend this line of work by studying how well steering for faithfulness generalizes across cue types, datasets, and methods of constructing the steering vector for three models (Gemma-3 4B, Qwen-3.5 9B, Gemma-3 12B) in a cued question-answering setting. While steering reliably increases cue acknowledgment for only the largest model (Gemma-3 12B), we find that when steering is effective, its effect generalizes broadly across cue types and datasets--in cross-cue and cross-dataset analyses, effect size is determined primarily by the evaluation setting, rather than the vector's train setting. How the vector is built also matters little--four construction methods, including one whose optimization target mentions no specific cue, yield similar effect sizes. Finally, we consider the possibility that steering promotes the salience of the cue and causes greater cue use, rather than targeting verbalization behaviors. However, we find no evidence for this--steering leaves the rate of cue use roughly unchanged while reducing hidden cue use, i.e., cue use that is not acknowledged.

</details>


### [127] [A Generalized-Bayes Perspective on Counterfactual Explanations: Posterior-Based Decision-Making and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29077)
*Keita Kinjo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从广义贝叶斯框架出发，证明了基于距离最小化的反事实解释（CE）等价于带有距离先验的Gibbs后验的最大后验（MAP）估计，并提出了一种统一框架——距离先验广义贝叶斯CE（DP-GBCE）。该框架引入了多种决策规则，包括贝叶斯决策和风险厌恶的CVaR-CE，并通过贝叶斯模型权重混合多个模型的后验分布来处理模型多重性问题。最后定义了评估指标，并通过模拟数据和Google Trends数据实验量化了不同决策规则间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释（CE）通常被定义为距离最小化问题，但这一公式化的理论基础长期缺乏严格的数学论证。作者希望从贝叶斯视角为距离最小化CE提供理论支撑，并在此框架下扩展出更丰富的决策规则以突破传统方法仅依赖距离度量的局限。

Method: 论文将距离最小化CE与Gibbs后验的MAP估计建立数学等价关系，提出DP-GBCE统一框架。在此框架下引入三种决策规则：MAP估计、最小化期望决策损失的贝叶斯决策、以及风险厌恶的CVaR-CE。同时利用贝叶斯模型平均方法混合多个模型的后验分布，并定义了评估个体CE和后验分布整体质量的指标。

Result: 通过模拟数据和Google Trends数据的实验，论文验证了DP-GBCE框架的有效性，并量化了MAP、贝叶斯决策和CVaR-CE等不同决策规则之间的权衡关系，展示了不同规则在不同场景下的适用性。

Conclusion: 该研究为反事实解释提供了坚实的贝叶斯理论基础，将传统距离最小化方法统一到广义贝叶斯框架中，并扩展了CE的决策规则空间，为处理模型多元性和风险敏感场景提供了新工具。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CEs) enhance the interpretability of machine learning models by identifying the smallest change to an input required to obtain a desired output. Although CEs are conventionally formulated as a distance-minimization problem, the theoretical basis of this formulation has received limited attention. We show that a distance-minimization-based CE is mathematically equivalent to the maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimate of a Gibbs posterior within the generalized Bayes framework, specifically when a distance-based prior is used. We call this formulation the Distance-Prior Generalized Bayes CE (DP-GBCE). Building on this posterior perspective, we introduce two decision rules beyond MAP within a unified framework: a Bayes decision that minimizes expected decision loss and CVaR-CE, a risk-averse decision rule. We also propose an extension that uses Bayesian model weights to mix the posterior distributions of multiple models, thereby accounting for model multiplicity, where several models have comparable predictive performance. Finally, we define metrics for evaluating both individual CEs and the posterior distribution as a whole, and use experiments on simulated data and Google Trends data to quantify the trade-offs among the decision rules.

</details>


### [128] [Harnessing the Wisdom of LLM Crowds through Complementarity-Driven Iterative Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29087)
*Yanbin Fang,Xuan Wei,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WILC框架，将大规模语言模型的集体智能重新概念化为接力式互补协作：每个后继模型根据前驱模型输出的特定瓶颈被选择，通过迭代反思-精炼和双门控模型选择机制实现协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在组合多个LLM时预先固定协作方式，忽视了复杂问题解决中模型互补性的动态性和状态依赖性，未能充分释放多模型协作的集体智能潜力。

Method: 提出WILC框架，包含两个设计原则：(1) 迭代反思-精炼的状态保持工作流，让模型诊断并改进先前输出；(2) 互补性驱动的模型选择，通过双门控机制（前瞻性互补匹配PCF选择适应当前瓶颈的工人模型，后验互补增益PCG评估所选转换是否改善解决方案）来控制模型间的转换。

Result: 在四个多样化基准上的实验表明，WILC优于现有方法（包括单模型自我精炼、集成方法和查询路由方法）。在标准化定价假设下，WILC以约7倍更低的每查询预估成本匹配GPT-5.2的平均基准性能，并支持自托管部署以实现数据主权。

Conclusion: 本研究将智慧众包理论从静态聚合拓展到序列式AI互补协作，为多AI协调提供了可迁移的设计原则。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in enterprise settings, yet individual models remain bounded by model-specific capability limitations. These heterogeneous boundaries pose a deployment challenge, but also create an opportunity: strategically coordinating multiple LLMs may unlock collective intelligence exceeding any single model. Existing approaches fix how models are combined in advance, overlooking the dynamic, state-dependent role of complementarity in complex problem solving. Drawing on the wisdom-of-crowds paradigm, we reconceptualize collective LLM intelligence as relay-style complementarity: a sequential process in which each successor model is selected to address the specific bottleneck identified in its predecessor's output. To operationalize this, we propose WILC (Wisdom Integration of LLM Crowds), a framework grounded in two design principles. First, iterative reflection-and-refinement establishes a state-preserving workflow through which models diagnose and refine prior outputs. Second, complementarity-driven model selection governs transitions via a dual-gate mechanism: prospective complementarity fit (PCF) identifies the worker most suited to the current bottleneck, while posterior complementarity gain (PCG) evaluates whether the selected transition improves the evolving solution. Experiments across four diverse benchmarks show that WILC outperforms existing approaches, including single-model self-refinement, ensemble methods, and query-routing methods. Under standardized pricing assumptions, WILC matches the average benchmark performance of GPT-5.2 at roughly 7 times lower estimated per-query cost, while facilitating data sovereignty through self-hosted deployment. This study extends wisdom-of-crowds theory from static aggregation to sequential AI complementarity and provides transferable design principles for multi-AI coordination.

</details>


### [129] [CAGE: Certified Authorization under Typed-Return Uncertainty for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29190)
*Blaise Delattre,Cong Wang,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAGE框架，用于认证工具型LLM智能体在绑定错误和数值漂移下的动作授权安全性，解决了分类和数值通道分别认证不可组合的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运行时权限门通常只基于观测到的返回值和动作进行授权，但对返回结果如何绑定到其来源（provenance）的小错误缺乏保护。作者发现分别对分类通道和数值通道进行安全认证并不能组合成联合安全，需要一种能够同时覆盖两类扰动的认证方法。

Method: CAGE框架直接对联合邻域（一个可容许的绑定错误加有界数值漂移）进行认证，通过精确枚举离散分支并对每个分支内的连续扰动进行认证来实现。根据策略是否可执行，提供CAGE-Exact（直接认证策略本身）和CAGE-Lip/CAGE-RS（在明确可测的保真度假设下认证学习到的门）三种变体。

Result: 在合成数据、策略即代码、监管场景和真实交易场景中，CAGE能够消除精确逐点门所允许的错误授权（false allows），同时保留相当比例的决策可自主执行。

Conclusion: CAGE通过联合认证绑定错误和数值漂移的邻域，为工具型LLM智能体的运行时授权提供了更严格的安全保障，弥补了单独认证分类和数值通道在组合时的漏洞。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents act on typed tool returns, records pairing provenance and categorical fields with numerical values. Runtime permission gates generally authorize the observed return and action, leaving the decision unprotected against small errors in how the return was bound to its source. We ask whether a candidate action stays authorized over a declared neighborhood of plausible correctly bound returns: one admissible binding fault plus bounded numerical drift. We prove that certifying the categorical and numerical channels separately does not compose: perturbations that are safe on each channel alone can jointly turn the same action unsafe. CAGE certifies this joint neighborhood directly, enumerating the discrete branches exactly and certifying the continuous perturbation within each branch. Across synthetic, policy-as-code, regulatory, and real-transaction settings, CAGE removes the in-budget false allows that accurate pointwise gates admit, while keeping a useful fraction of decisions autonomous. When the policy is executable, CAGE-Exact certifies the policy itself; otherwise CAGE-Lip and CAGE-RS certify a learned gate under an explicit, measured fidelity assumption.

</details>


### [130] [MirrorCraft: Paired Evaluation under Hidden Rule Changes in Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29218)
*Jianxin Gao,Beini Hu,Runze Li,Wanli Peng,Ruohan Lei,Jinyuan Zhang,Linna Deng,Tianyi Yu,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MirrorCraft是一个配对基准测试集，用于评估大语言模型智能体在Minecraft中面对隐藏规则变化时的表现。每个Mirror世界复制对应的Vanilla世界但修改部分服务器端规则，通过规则干预效应（RIE）量化规则变化对智能体性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Minecraft基准测试都在固定游戏机制下评估LLM智能体，无法反映当熟悉的配方、掉落物等规则改变时智能体能否继续取得进展，缺乏对规则变化适应能力的系统评估。

Method: 构建配对基准MirrorCraft：每个Mirror世界是Vanilla世界的副本并修改选定规则。包含五个受控生物群系、六套规则、三个进展目标、两个模型家族和六种智能体配置。使用确定性进展里程碑和成功率评估任务进展，并用RIE衡量配对世界间的性能差异。

Result: 实验表明隐藏规则变化对不同规则套件的影响差异显著。在未提供规则描述时，ReAct在Mirror世界取得最高综合得分；提供精确规则后，三个目标的平均进展和完成率均有适度提升。

Conclusion: MirrorCraft将Minecraft评估从固定机制扩展到规则可变的场景，为研究智能体在当前世界规则与熟悉规则不同时如何利用游戏结果进行适应性决策提供了受控实验环境。

Abstract: With the prosperity of the large language models (LLMs), it has become an interesting topic: how do LLM-based agents work in Minecraft? Unfortunately, most existing benchmarks evaluate them under fixed game mechanics. High performance in these settings does not show whether an agent can continue making progress when familiar recipes, drops, and other rules change. In this paper, we introduce MirrorCraft, a paired benchmark for evaluating agents under hidden rule changes in Minecraft. Each Mirror world is a copy of its paired Vanilla world, with selected server-side rules modified by the corresponding datapack. Terrain, spawn, resource placement, objective, interface, and action budget remain matched within every Vanilla-Mirror pair. MirrorCraft includes five controlled biomes, six rule suites, three progression objectives, two model families, and six agent configurations under a shared Mineflayer interface. We evaluate task progress with deterministic advancement milestones and success rate and use the Rule Intervention Effect (RIE) to measure the performance change between matched Vanilla and Mirror worlds. The experiments show that hidden rule changes have strongly different effects across suites. Among the configurations evaluated without rule descriptions, ReAct achieves the highest pooled Mirror score. Providing the exact rules yields modest gains in average progress and completion across all three objectives. MirrorCraft extends Minecraft evaluation beyond fixed mechanics and provides a controlled setting for studying how agents use gameplay outcomes when the rules of the current world differ from familiar ones.

</details>


### [131] [Don't Mix Rewards, Mix Policies: Policy Decomposition and Optimization for Multi-Reward RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29246)
*Ruiming Liang,Yi Zhong,Yizhen Yuan,Yinan Zheng,Tianyi Tan,Tianyue Wang,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Xianyuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRISM是一个基于策略空间分解与组合的多奖励强化学习框架，通过分别优化一系列独立的正向策略和一个全局负向策略来避免多奖励冲突，并在推理时实现灵活的策略组合控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要适应不同人类价值观和使用场景，多奖励强化学习变得日益重要。然而，多个奖励优化目标之间可能存在权衡甚至冲突，导致对齐代价（alignment tax）问题更加严重，产生不稳定和低效的后训练。

Method: PRISM采用策略空间分解与组合的思想，不对多个奖励进行直接组合优化，而是分别优化一组独立的正向策略（每个对应一个奖励）和一个全局的负向策略，从而缓解多奖励优化中的潜在冲突，并在推理阶段通过灵活的策略组合实现可控性。

Result: 在科学推理、工具使用推理和有用性-安全性对齐三个实验任务上，PRISM一致性地优于现有的多奖励强化学习基线方法，并且额外提供了推理时的偏好控制能力。

Conclusion: PRISM通过策略空间分解与组合的方法有效解决了多奖励RL中的冲突问题，在提升性能的同时赋予了推理阶段的偏好可控性，为LLM多奖励后训练提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) are expected not just to answer correctly, but to adapt their behavior to different human values and use cases. As a result, multi-reward reinforcement learning (RL) has become an increasingly important problem for LLMs, where each reward captures a different aspect of desired behavior. However, optimizing with multiple rewards suffers from a more severe alignment tax issue, where different optimization objectives can trade off or even conflict with each other, leading to unstable and inefficient post-training. In this work, we propose PRISM, a new multi-reward RL framework built upon the idea of policy-space decomposition and composition. Instead of compositing different rewards, PRISM optimizes a set of standalone positive policies and a global negative policy. This alleviates the potential conflict during multi-reward policy optimization, while enabling controllability during inference by flexible policy composition. Experiments on scientific reasoning, tool-use reasoning, and helpfulness-safety alignment show that PRISM consistently outperforms existing multi-reward RL baselines, with extra controllability for inference-time preference control.

</details>


### [132] [Tool Specifications Matter: Uncovering and Mitigating Safety Risks in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29254)
*Minghui Pan,Jiayuxuan Yang,Yuanyuan Yuan,Yu Jiang,Zhenpeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现schema格式的工具规范会削弱LLM的内部拒绝信号，导致智能体安全性下降；提出的SafeKeep方法将安全判断与工具执行解耦，在推理时使用展平的文本工具规范评估请求，同时保留原始schema格式用于执行，显著提升了对有害请求的拒绝率和抵御提示注入攻击的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为智能体部署时安全性显著下降，但其根源尚未被充分理解。作者通过白盒表征分析发现，schema格式化的工具规范是导致智能体安全性退化的主要原因，因为它削弱了模型内部的拒绝信号。

Method: SafeKeep是一种推理时的安全防护方法，核心思想是将安全判断与工具执行解耦：在评估请求时使用展平（flattened）的文本工具规范，而在执行时保留原始的schema格式规范。该方法适用于白盒和黑盒模型。

Result: 在两个代表性基准和四个LLM上，SafeKeep将有害请求的平均拒绝率从23.8%提升至70.6%，将观察级提示注入的攻击成功率从25.6%降至2.5%，并在性能上优于现有防护方法且不损害任务处理能力。

Conclusion: SafeKeep有效缓解了LLM智能体的安全性退化问题，通过简单而有效的解耦策略大幅提升了安全防护效果，且不牺牲任务处理能力，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: AI agents extend large language models (LLMs) with external tools, enabling them to perform complex tasks and translate model outputs into consequential real-world actions. Yet LLMs often become substantially less safe when deployed as agents, and the source of this degradation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we identify schema-formatted tool specifications as a primary source of agent safety degradation and show, through white-box representation analysis, that they weaken the model's internal refusal signals and contribute to unsafe tool execution. Building on this finding, we propose SafeKeep, an inference-time safeguard that decouples safety judgment from tool execution: it assesses requests using flattened textual tool specifications while retaining the original schema-formatted specifications for execution. Across two representative benchmarks and four LLMs, including both white-box and black-box models, SafeKeep increases the average refusal rate for harmful requests from 23.8% to 70.6% and reduces the average attack success rate under observation-level prompt injection from 25.6% to 2.5%. It also outperforms existing safeguards and preserves task-handling capability. We release the code and data at https://github.com/snowcatsmoking/SafeKeep .

</details>


### [133] [MAGA: Multi-Platform Self-Fusion of GUI Agents via Structured Action Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29320)
*Hang Yan,Zhangxuan GU,Beitong Zhou,Jiaxuan Chen,Runze Li,Yusong Hu,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGA方法，通过根据结构化动作的正确性重新分配训练信号，将领域特定的GUI智能体合并为统一的跨环境策略，在8B和更大规模模型上均取得最佳平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GUI智能体通常是领域特定的（仅针对移动、网页或桌面环境），限制了部署和用户体验。需要将专业模型整合为单一跨环境策略，但现有的权重合并和在线策略蒸馏方法存在不足：权重合并在专家分歧时会破坏可执行动作，而在线策略蒸馏将所有响应令牌等同对待，忽略了动作令牌是智能体与环境交互的唯一接口。

Method: 提出MAGA框架，核心思想是根据结构化动作的正确性重新分配训练信号：抑制不必要或无效的蒸馏信号，专注于错误动作的学习。此外，引入训练专属提示（training-only hint）来优化领域特定教师提供的监督信号，同时不改变学生输入。

Result: 在两种模型规模下，MAGA均取得了最高的平均成功率，在8B规模下比最强基线高出2.0%，且平均性能与教师模型几乎持平。

Conclusion: MAGA通过聚焦动作令牌和重新分配蒸馏信号，有效解决了跨环境GUI智能体的多领域整合问题，实现了接近领域特定教师模型的统一策略性能。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents based on large language models are increasingly deployed across mobile, web, and desktop environments. However, existing agents are typically domain-specific, limiting the deployment and user experience. This motivates the consolidation of specialized models into a single cross-environment policy. Weight merging directly merges domain-specific experts but can corrupt executable actions under expert disagreement, while on-policy distillation (OPD) avoids conflicting teacher supervision yet still treats all response tokens equally during distillation, ignoring that action tokens are the only interface between the environment and the agent. To address this, We introduce MAGA that re-allocates training signal according to the structured action. Based on the correctness of the generated action, it suppresses unnecessary or invalid distillation signals and focuses learning on erroneous actions. Besides, a training-only hint optimizes the supervision signal provided by domain-specific teachers without changing the student input. Across two model scales, MAGA achieves the highest mean success rate, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.0% at 8B and achieves almost the same average performance with teachers.

</details>


### [134] [Beyond Component Testing: Validating Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29405)
*Fabio Orazio Mirto,Luca D'Agati,Giuseppe Tricomi,Stefano Silvestri,Francesco Longo,Antonio Puliafito,Giovanni Merlino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了257篇论文，提出一个五维分类框架（行为、安全、时间、监管、多智能体），系统刻画了智能体AI系统的验证问题，指出行为评估相对成熟，而时间有效性、运行时证据维护、监管可读性和开放多智能体系统保障仍不成熟，并提出了以生命周期为导向的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统通过多步轨迹运作（结合规划、工具使用、记忆、交互和适应），其行为依赖于决策如何在时间和变化环境中展开，这使得传统组件测试和单次输入-输出评估不再适用，需要系统性地重新审视验证问题。

Method: 综合了257篇涵盖智能体评估、软件保证、信息物理系统、运行时监控和监管指南的论文，提出五维分类学（行为、安全、时间、监管、多智能体）来分析现有方法并揭示覆盖缺口；同时通过三个跨领域案例研究（医疗、工业运营、智能移动系统）进行操作化说明。

Result: 行为评估相对成熟；时间有效性、运行时证据维护、监管可读性和开放式多智能体系统保障仍存在明显不足；三个安全关键领域案例验证了五维分类在实践中的重现性。

Conclusion: 智能体AI的可信部署取决于在上下文中验证轨迹而非孤立评估组件；研究议程聚焦于有界自治规范、对抗性轨迹生成、运行时监控和可审计的证据结构。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems act through multi-step trajectories that combine planning, tool use, memory, interaction, and adaptation. This behavior stretches validation practice beyond component testing and one-shot input--output evaluation, because acceptable system behavior now depends on how decisions unfold over time and under changing environmental conditions. This survey synthesizes 257 papers spanning agent evaluation, software assurance, cyber-physical systems, runtime monitoring, and regulatory guidance in order to characterize the validation problem for agentic systems. The review is organized around a five-dimension taxonomy covering behavioral, safety, temporal, regulatory, and multi-agent concerns, and uses that taxonomy to map current approaches and expose recurrent coverage gaps. The analysis shows that behavioral evaluation is comparatively mature, while temporal validity, runtime evidence maintenance, regulatory legibility, and open-ended multi-agent systems assurance remain under-developed. Three cross-domain case studies (medical care, industrial operations, smart-mobility systems) provide operational illustrations of how the five taxonomy dimensions recur in safety-critical settings, grounded in the failure patterns documented in the reviewed literature. The paper concludes with a lifecycle-oriented research agenda centered on bounded-autonomy specifications, adversarial trajectory generation, runtime monitoring, and audit-ready evidence structures. The central claim is that trustworthy deployment of agentic AI depends on validating trajectories in context rather than assessing isolated components alone.

</details>


### [135] [ModelEquivBench: Certifying Multi-Relational Evaluation of LLM-Generated Optimization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29431)
*Penglin Zhu,Jungang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ModelEquivBench，一个可认证、多关系的大语言模型优化建模评估系统，输出E0-E6七级语义画像而非单一等价值，提供可独立复核的证据，并在173个基础问题上对三个模型快照进行评测，揭示粗糙基线无法区分的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方式将生成的优化模型与其标准答案简化为单一等价/不等价判定或执行成功率，这些标签既不可独立验证，也无法忠实反映两个模型公式在多种不同意义上的一致性。

Method: 提出ModelEquivBench系统，定义E0-E6七级语义关系（模型构建与精确摄入、表示对齐、可行集关系、目标序等价、最优值等价、优化器集等价），每级判定附带可复核证据（可重放轨迹、精确有理证书、反例见证），对不支持的情况输出UNKNOWN/N/A而非猜测，对前置条件缺失输出ABSENT。在173个基础问题上对三个模型快照进行无修复协议评测。

Result: 三个模型（GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Qwen3.5-397B-A17B）分别有49、35、25个单元包含可执行候选但在至少一个支持关系上被认证为否定；分别有25、8、18个结构性拒绝发生在E2已验证映射可行集相等的情况下。三个模型在不同阶段失败，无法简化为单一准确率分数。

Conclusion: 单一准确率分数无法概括LLM优化建模能力，ModelEquivBench的多关系语义画像能够揭示模型在不同语义维度上的差异化表现，为更细粒度、可验证的评估提供新范式。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate optimization models from natural language, but existing evaluation often reduces a generated model and its ground truth to a single equivalent/not-equivalent verdict or an execution-success rate--labels that are neither independently checkable nor faithful to the multiple distinct senses in which two formulations can agree. We present ModelEquivBench, a certifying, multi-relational evaluation system that reports a per-pair semantic profile E0--E6: model construction and exact ingestion (E0), verified representation alignment (E1), same-space and projected feasible-set relations (E2, E3), objective-order equivalence (E4), optimal-value equality (E5), and optimizer-set equivalence (E6). Each decided entry carries relation-appropriate, independently re-checkable evidence: replayable traces or explicit maps for E0--E1, exact-rational certificates for positive E2--E6 conclusions, and explicit witnesses for supported negatives. Incomplete mapping search, unsupported structure, and resource limits produce typed UNKNOWN or N/A outcomes rather than guesses, while unmet prerequisites are reported as ABSENT. Using ModelEquivBench to evaluate three model snapshots--GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Qwen3.5-397B-A17B--on the same frozen cohort of 173 base problems (346 cells per model) under a no-repair protocol, the resulting profiles expose distinctions that coarse baselines do not represent: 49, 35, and 25 cells contain executable candidates that are nevertheless certified negative on at least one supported relation, and 25, 8, and 18 structural rejections occur on pairs for which E2 certifies mapped feasible-set equality under a verified map. The three model snapshots fail at different stages of the profile and therefore cannot be meaningfully reduced to a single accuracy score.

</details>


### [136] [Beyond Retrieval: Analytic Memory for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29440)
*Zhoujin Tian,Yao Tian,Hao Zhang,Cheng Chen,Yakun Li,Lei Zhang,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaMM框架，在传统检索记忆之外引入"分析记忆"（analytic memory）概念，通过从多模态交互中提取属性-值观察并物化为可查询结构，支持过滤、聚合、排序和时间比较等操作，在两个基准上分别提升11.3%和7.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期多模态记忆系统主要侧重于检索记忆，即通过摘要和索引组织交互历史来返回查询相关信息，但缺乏对跨交互累积观测数据进行计算（如过滤、聚合、排序、时间比较）的能力，需要一种互补的记忆抽象来支持分析性查询。

Method: 提出AdaMM框架：1) 从对话、图像和上下文元数据中提取带溯源链接的属性-值观察；2) 自动发现重复出现的字段结构而不依赖应用定义的模式；3) 物化这些结构以供分析访问；4) 推理时使用记忆感知的计划器将查询分解为检索和分析操作，并将每个操作路由到适当的工具。

Result: 在MemEye和MemGallery两个长期多模态记忆基准上，AdaMM分别将性能提升了11.3%和7.3%。

Conclusion: AdaMM证明了检索记忆与分析记忆的联合支持能够更全面地满足长期多模态记忆需求，通过自动提取和物化结构化观察数据，显著提升了记忆系统在分析性查询上的表现。

Abstract: Long-term multimodal memory must support not only retrieving relevant information but also computing over observations accumulated across interactions. Existing systems largely emphasize \emph{retrieval memory}, organizing interaction histories through summaries and indexes to return query-relevant information at multiple granularities, from high-level abstractions to underlying records. In this paper, we formulate \emph{analytic memory} as a complementary abstraction that organizes recurring multimodal observations into queryable structures supporting filtering, aggregation, ranking, and temporal comparison. We present AdaMM, a framework that jointly supports retrieval and analytic memory. Rather than relying on application-defined schemas, AdaMM extracts provenance-linked attribute-value observations from dialogue, images, and contextual metadata, discovers recurring field structures, and materializes them for analytical access. At inference time, a memory-aware planner decomposes queries into retrieval and analytic operations and routes each operation to the appropriate tools. Experiments on two long-term multimodal memory benchmarks, MemEye and MemGallery, show that AdaMM improves performance by up to 11.3\% and 7.3\%, respectively.

</details>


### [137] [Self-Play Meets Skill Evolution: Self-Evolving Search Agents that Pose, Solve, and Remember](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29468)
*Zenghuang Fu,Zhaoyang Li,Qiuyuan Ai,Haoyu Wu,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Ante Wang,Guannan He,Changwei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SESA（自进化技能增强智能体），通过将程序性记忆作为自玩搜索的可进化状态，让任务生成和技能记忆双向共进化，在多个开放域和多跳问答基准上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
